## 54. Claude follow-up corrections of GDR-CR53-007 and GDR-CR53-008, 2026-10-28

### 54.1 Scope and method

After the section-53 push the owner instructed Claude to "proceed to fix all
findings you identified". Section 53 left two findings documented rather than
corrected: GDR-CR53-007 (the accepted SYN runtime's due-exit coalescing) and
GDR-CR53-008 (nine reachable engine edges without a pin). This section
corrects both. Pinning the edges exposed one new P2 defect, GDR-CR54-002,
which is also corrected. At the start the remote, local branch and worktree
all matched `37bce7f2`; Codex had pushed nothing new. Same branch and
worktree; no other branch.

Every run used the designated worktree, network-denied. No QC page, provider
account, browser session or real data was contacted. Every exercised row is
invented. `docs/ACTION_PLAN_2026-08-20.md` and `docs/SESSION_HANDOFF.md` were
not edited.

This round changes `simulation.py`, a native-closure module, for the first
time since the retained `c589b3dd` custody. 54.2 states what that does and
does not change.

### 54.2 GDR-CR53-007: SYN due exits keep their own dates

Section 53 understated the reachability. The native algorithm and every
accepted fixture, scenario and release payload still never issue
`schedule_invalidation`, but `recovery.RecoveryEngine` accepts it as an allowed
public command, so an offline command journal reaches the premature
liquidation. A trim due on session 1 and an invalidation effective on
session 3 became one exit on session 1 for all 49 shares, in either order of
arrival.

Fix in `Simulation._schedule` and the new `_combine_due`: requests that the
next possible 10:00 exit would reach anyway merge exactly as before (earliest
session, largest quantity, highest-priority reason). A request dated after
that horizon stays in the due record's `later` list, visible in
`scheduled_exits`. It joins the due exit at the first `_schedule` or
`execute_due_exits` whose horizon reaches it. A sale that exhausts the
earlier request hands the record to the later rows. Splits scale them, and a
full sale or cash terminal removes them with the record.

Without a later-dated request the state, snapshots and orders are unchanged.
At `37bce7f2` and after the fix, the fixture stream (`8f35d56a...`) and the
base and stress `run_example` results are byte-identical. `run_fixture_report`
differs only in code-derived identity fields (`code_sha256`, the epoch, input
and chain hashes, and the two changed files' digests).

Native identity: the offline native audit ZIP named in 33.4,
`5d081569a901ec5ed3fa0a38b282050cb1dddc276482dee4dbd0e6a60aaa7230` (292,740
bytes), reproduces at `37bce7f2`. After the fix it is
`fa562a43d49f59e5e3305c3d99cb2d3537bf0045a494504c3f5e5711fd8ae3e6` (293,994
bytes, 22 members); only `simulation.py` differs. The retained `c589b3dd`
custody is untouched: recovery project `c6b9443a...`, prepared runtime
`5d1dc656...`, audit ZIP `5d081569...` and fixture `8f35d56a...`. The
`GDR-Mia-base372-recovery-authorized-20261011` authorization still names that
custody. Its runtime contains the unreachable defect, which its carrier cannot
reach. A native attempt from the current source would be a new candidate,
needing independent review and owner direction first. No carrier, project or
release was regenerated, saved or uploaded.

### 54.3 GDR-CR54-002: cash terminal after an unresolved split

Pinning MSAA13 showed that section 53 mislabelled it as a conservative mutant.
After a fractional (1.5) split that the engine leaves unresolved, a $7 cash
terminal paid 495 x 7 = 3,465, the same as with no split. It cleared the
unresolved flag, and the replay reported `complete_software_replay` `True`
with `comparison_parity_blocked` `False` and no blockers. The entitled share
count after the split is unknown (742.5 shares before cash in lieu), so the
payout and the clean result were both wrong. A split refused for open orders
(`split_requires_reconciled_orders`) has the same shape.

Fix in `MultiSessionReplay._apply_action`: a `terminal_cash` on a security
whose unresolved state is either split reason takes the existing unresolved
terminal path. The security is marked terminal and keeps its exposure as
`unsupported_or_unresolved_terminal_economics`; no receivable is created; and
the parity row `unsupported_terminal_comparison_schedule` is recorded. A
resolved split is unaffected (a 2:1 split still pays 990 x 7). An exit made
moot by the payout (`invalidation_exit_outside_replay_window`,
`required_exit_outside_replay_window`) is still cleared; MSAA13 pins that.
`test_cash_terminal_after_an_unresolved_split_stays_unresolved` fails without
the fix, and both FIX54 mutants are caught.

### 54.4 GDR-CR53-008: the nine engine edges are pinned

New class `EdgePinTests` in `test_multi_session.py`, plus a `postpone` helper
that publishes one cloned security's revision a day later so it enters on a
later session:

| Mutant | Pin | Construction |
|---|---|---|
| MSE14 | `test_entry_account_counts_an_outstanding_strategy_receivable` | A 9.9 mark leaves NAV 99,945.55 and a 494-share second entry; a $0.20 dividend receivable restores 495 |
| MSC9, MSC11 | `test_gross_cap_trims_the_eleventh_position_in_sorted_order` | Ten dividends make room for an eleventh entry; at a close of 11 the 50% gross cap trims `INVENTED-SEC-9` by 183 |

## 56. SPY admission-budget reconciliation without rule changes, 2026-10-28

### 56.1 Diagnosed cause and bounded correction

After completing the counter-review and local correction above, implement
only the requested budget-parity repair. GDR-RP56-001 (P2 / corrected) is a
false alarm in `MultiSessionReplay._fill_orders`: `_spy_entry` maximizes whole
shares against the decision limit plus worst-case fees, but completion judged
its actual cash residual against the cheaper eventual execution price.
Released price-protection reserves were therefore mistaken for underallocation.

The original plan's sections 5 and 6 require limit-based sizing, fees reserved
separately, no topping up after fills, matched allocated budgets and retained
idle cash. Preserve those rules and the fixed SPY-53 inputs. Do not enlarge
the 92-share comparator order to 93, change the fixture, alter impact/fees,
relax the funding/timing/partial-fill guards or silently widen a tolerance.

For a completely filled comparator entry, reconcile exact decimal amounts:

```
committed = filled_shares * submitted_limit + cumulative_commission
whole_share_rounding_cash = source_budget - committed
released_price_reserve = committed - actual_entry_spending
actual_idle_cash = whole_share_rounding_cash + released_price_reserve
next_share_limit_cost = submitted_limit + commission(q+1) - commission(q)
```

Valid limit-based allocation requires nonnegative rounding cash strictly below
the next-share limit cost, and nonnegative released price reserve. Equality
at the next-share boundary remains a mismatch. The two kinds of idle cash
are recorded separately in the tranche reconciliation; released reserves are
not relabelled whole-share rounding. Genuine funding, delayed/unmatched fills
and partial execution remain visible blockers. No second order/top-up or cash
injection is created.

The unchanged fixed recipe reconciles as follows (invented dollars):

| Mode | Source budget | SPY shares | Limit-rounding cash | Released price reserve | Actual idle cash |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Base | 4,954.9500 | 92 | 29.1900 | 46.3220 | 75.5120 |
| Stress | 4,959.9000 | 92 | 33.1400 | 41.4460 | 74.5860 |

The handoff retains `gdr.pre-qc-handoff.v2` and its invented recipe, but its
status becomes `invented_replays_reconciled_external_gates_closed`. The old
invented-budget blocker is removed from the current remaining-work list;
real-data funding/timing/fill parity is still unverified. Original economic
decisions, source-rights/PIT flags, native verification, independent review,
empirical and QC/trading authority stay closed. Historical handoffs are not
rewritten; exact source-hash reconstruction distinguishes each epoch.

### 56.2 Final verification and publication handoff

SPY correction commit:
`69f2a4a24d5521c93bc7cdfc47f5064f461b5bd0`.
The additive tranche `entry_budget_reconciliation` is `None` until the entry
order fills completely. Its completed form retains order ID, original filled
quantity and limit, cumulative commission, committed limit cost, admission
rounding cash, released price reserve, actual cash residual, next-share limit
cost and `within_rounding`. It is a proof of this invented admission, not
proof of funding/timing/partial-fill parity or a later share basis. It remains
unchanged across a subsequent SPY split. A cancelled/refused/unfilled entry
never receives a completed proof.

The former SPY-53 expectation of a blocker was obsolete because it mixed
admission and execution prices. Replace it with stronger expected cash,
quantity and separate-reserve assertions; preserve every real refusal guard.
Ten new behavioral methods cover fixed base/stress execution, coherent
one-share undersizing, exact next-share equality, negative limit headroom
despite positive actual-price cash, commission-floor crossovers at
199/200 and 399/400 shares, favorable price changes without top-up, cumulative
partial-fill fees, funding/zero-share refusals, above-limit non-execution and
the retained admission audit after a split. Six finally-restored in-memory
mutants are caught with zero errors: the old actual-price basis, always
accepting, accepting next-share equality, dropping nonnegative headroom,
omitting incremental fees and erasing the released reserve. The ten new
methods pass in 5.317 s; the earlier ten-method selection including two
updated originals passed in 7.992 s.

| Sharadar Bundle 5Y | Filing fundamentals, EOD stock/fund prices, actions and active/inactive reference context. | No issuer-guidance revision or intraday bid/ask product established. Five-year ownership alone does not meet the draft's six-common-year planning floor. Do not buy additional fundamentals to address a different missing data type. |

### 57.4 Exact remaining blockers and practical next step

The original plan permits declared current-vintage and last-update-censored
historical exploration with `point_in_time_data=false`. Immutable every-version
and original first-delivery proof are PIT/confirmation prerequisites, not a
universal bar to every exploratory backtest. Qualify older statements of the
guidance-history gap accordingly. An exploratory study still needs actual
usable current/predecessor coverage, disclosed availability/reconstruction
assumptions, rights and a frozen authorized outcome scope. No such dataset
audit or scope freeze is manufactured here.

The unresolved blockers are:

1. An account-entitled issuer-guidance source with usable comparable FY EPS
   and revenue disclosures/predecessors; documented time, confirmation,
   correction/withdrawal and reconstruction semantics. For PIT/confirmation,
   additionally prove original versions/availability. A product name alone
   proves neither evidence class.
2. Historical consolidated stock and SPY NBBO plus synchronized minute
   volume, with actual usable common dates, inactive identifiers, conditions,
   units and normalization. The draft proposes at least six common years
   ending no later than August 31, 2026; five rolling years do not alone meet
   that floor. No protected dates or later outcomes are opened to extend it.
3. Applicable account/partner/exchange permission for the intended personal
   research, raw/reconstructible audit retention and specifically identified
   private external processing, including after the paid term where needed.
   Resolve contractual ambiguity before data acquisition/upload.
4. Authentic dated identity/sector/action/terminal and settlement calendars,
   historical fees and declared price normalization. Existing subscriptions
   may cover parts, but completeness has not been measured.
5. The real-data/native execution adapter and order/cash/NAV/comparator parity
   validation, plus independent review of new section-55/56 fixes. The fixed
   invented SPY-budget false alarm is closed, not a remaining purchase need.
6. Explicit research/economic/date/look freeze and eventual separately scoped
   evaluation authority. All eleven original null decisions remain; current
   no-QC instruction prevents any native launch now.

The next purchase-free evidence step is a provider/account response or the
applicable contract identifying existing guidance/NBBO add-ons and answering
the exact history, timestamp, removal and retention/processing questions
above. No response was sent or agreement accepted. Only if existing access
cannot meet these requirements should additional guidance/NBBO coverage be
considered; Advanced and Corporate Guidance names alone are insufficient.

## 58. Connected USB FirstRate Data structural assessment, 2026-11-03

### 58.1 Owner scope, physical inventory and integrity limit

Owner: "i just connected a usb disk to the computer. examin whether the data
would be of any use here". This authorizes a local read-only usefulness and
structure inspection, not an empirical evaluation, unrestricted import,
source/policy change, QC access, purchase or publication. Start at clean
local `f95b84e5d26f20f429a1bd1f3d4d500f93f4b2ff`, one known local notes
commit ahead of live matching remote
`7d63314472b4462e153dde82b0bf71c848a253fa`; mandatory worktree, branch and
origin unchanged. Preserve the unpushed section-57 notes.

One external physical SANDISK disk is mounted at `/Volumes/SANDISK` with
`firstrate/market`, `firstrate/fundamentals` and `firstrate/manifest.json`.
The manifest contains 769 entries totalling 324,283,728,284 bytes (324.284
decimal GB, 302.013 GiB); there are 770 regular files including the manifest,
and no symlinks under `firstrate`. All 769 entries resolve to files on the
disk with matching declared sizes. Every manifest `ok` value is true, which
is retained downloader metadata, not a new checksum certification. The
manifest's naive `fetched` strings span October 17, 2026, 17:05:02–20:13:39;
no timezone, original data availability or subscriber identity is inferred.
Source hostnames in the manifest are `firstratedata.com`.

Stock and ETF directories contain 388 and 316 files, respectively. Other
directories cover indices, futures, FX, crypto and fundamentals; those are
inventoried only as context and do not add hypotheses to this lane. Only
documentation, ticker metadata, bounded ZIP central directories and CSV
headers are inspected. No data-value, earnings-result or price/action row is
opened or emitted. No extraction, execution of disk files, unverified
deserialization, provider API, backtest, outcome join or QC access occurs.
No agent command writes to the USB. The two `API_Access` files are not opened;
manifest download URLs are neither emitted nor visited. No credentials or
private access links enter the repository.

`c681af584244d38e3f9dea2ff765cc2c991cb383107a3e542115a4338ee4cdf8`.
The two small market README files also match their manifest MD5s. No full
checksum/CRC pass is claimed for the 324 GB collection or compressed payloads.

Unadjusted 1-minute/daily, split-only and split/dividend-adjusted variants are present. | Candidate raw execution-price, minute-volume and daily valuation inputs; not bid/ask/NBBO. Prefer raw bars plus separately verified action accounting; never double-count cash dividends with dividend-adjusted prices. |
| SPY | Ticker metadata lists January 3, 2000–October 16, 2026. ZIP inventory contains `SPY_full_1min_UNADJUSTED.txt` (249,648,453 uncompressed bytes) and `SPY_divs.txt`. | Long comparator bar/volume history is physically present. Metadata/member existence does not prove all sessions, conditions or synchronization. It does not fill the historical quote gap. |

ETF list: 5,100 symbols, February 26, 1999–October 16, 2026. | Far more than five years of nominal bar history, but not proof of six usable common years across guidance, quotes and the eligible universe. Ticker/name/first/last dates lack permanent identity, type, sector and exchange history. |

README identifies analyst consensus and reported EPS; release-time classification is coarse. | Earnings context, not management FY revenue/EPS guidance, comparable predecessor ranges, withdrawal/correction history or receipt/version evidence. |

Includes filing/fiscal dates and actual revenue/EPS fields. | Descriptive filed fundamentals; neither management outlooks nor a new baseline filter. A filing-date column does not establish original availability/PIT. |

No management-guidance product/category or corresponding guidance/version/
withdrawal/receipt-named member was found in the four inspected full
fundamental archives. This is a bounded inventory/schema result, not a claim
to have read every file's contents. No stock/ETF format inspected supplies
historical best bid/ask. Thus this disk can supply important existing bar,
volume, SPY and action candidates without another bar-data purchase, but
does not close either of the lane's two core signal/quote gaps.

Bundled market documentation specifies US Eastern EST/EDT, period-start
timestamps, share volume and omitted zero-trade minutes. A future adapter
must represent completed bars correctly, handle daylight saving, missing
minutes and regular sessions explicitly, and not fabricate prices or same-bar
fills. Unadjusted trade-volume inputs should not be silently replaced by
retroactively adjusted volume. The docs describe filtered, periodic ticker
additions; "Complete" is a product label, not an unbiased universe guarantee.

The provider's [FAQ](https://firstratedata.com/about/FAQ) separately confirms
incomplete delisted coverage, histories stored under recent tickers, distinct
daily/intraday construction, and split/dividend-only actions. Dated identity,
inactive-security coverage and terminal economics still need audit. No
current-ticker join is accepted as point-in-time mapping.

FirstRate's current public [licence](https://firstratedata.com/about/license)
supports perpetual downloaded-data use, research/model derivatives, and
service-provider access/copying/hosting on the subscriber's behalf. Raw
redistribution remains restricted and published research has an attribution
provision. This is substantially more positive retention/private-processing
evidence than section 57 established for some other providers. Bind these
downloads to the actual subscriber/purchase and applicable terms; possession
of a disk or an MD5 manifest alone is not that proof. Do not invent a separate
vendor sign-off requirement when the applicable licence already permits the
use. Nor does the licence grant this lane QC/run or trading authority.

## 59. USB unadjusted bar and corporate-action quality audit, 2026-11-03

### 59.1 Scope, owner provenance and date controls

Owner direction: "Proceed with the coverage and quality audit of the USB's
unadjusted stock/SPY bars and corporate-action files. Don't access QC or buy
anything, and tell me exactly what still blocks backtesting." This extends
58's metadata-only inspection to bounded local market/action scalar and
coverage checks. It does not authorize a strategy evaluation, return/outcome
join, parameter selection, provider acquisition, source-data repair, native
launch or protected-outcome inspection. Start at clean local
`918f405b91d675cac2c405051bb04866bcb77596`, retaining the two known local
notes commits ahead of live matching
`7d63314472b4462e153dde82b0bf71c848a253fa`. Mandatory worktree/branch/origin
remain unchanged; no new push is requested.

In response to the specific provenance question, the owner answered
"Purchased under my licence". Retain this as owner-reported licensed purchase,
not an independently inspected invoice. Together with the applicable public
FirstRate licence described in 58.3, this resolves the copy-provenance
question for this audit. Do not keep a blanket missing-licence blocker for
these USB files, require an invented additional approval, or transfer these
rights to guidance/NBBO from other vendors. Retain purchase/applicable-terms
provenance with a later dataset freeze. Earlier immutable audit receipts keep
their original conservative rights booleans; they are not rewritten into
permissions. No new contract is signed or account changed.

Numerical checks cover `2013-01-01 <= date < 2026-09-01`. Each row's date is
gated before financial-value decoding. Ascending bar members stop at their
first protected date; their later chronology and CRC are untested. Descending
action files require scanning date prefixes to EOF while excluding protected
financial lexemes. ZIP buffering/checksums can consume excluded raw bytes;
no protected financial field is parsed, emitted or used in an analysis.
Quality scripts compute scalar validity/coverage counts only, no returns,
profitability, candidate selection, trades or research-look receipt.

Scripts and aggregate/date-only receipts are retained locally under ignored
`artifacts/guidance_revision_drift/usb-quality-audit-20261103/`. They are audit
utilities, not additions to the accepted candidate/native source. No USB
writes, extraction, executable disk-code/pickle loading, API-access file,
credentials, private download links, provider calls, QC page/API/docs/quota/
Mia, purchases or account/resource changes. No raw dataset is committed.

### 59.2 Measured coverage and healthy sampled behaviour

| Audit | Actual scope | Evidence limit |
|---|---|---|
| Stock raw daily census | 32 containers: 26 active and six delisted, 517,458,054 compressed bytes. 16,477 member occurrences / 16,180 filename symbols; 23,659,724 eligible row occurrences. 15,641 members contain eligible rows; 836 contain none. Observed eligible endpoints January 2, 2013–August 31, 2026. | Complete selected inventory and eligible prefixes, not every later row, permanent security identity or an official-session completeness certificate. Empty eligible history may legitimately predate/postdate the window. |
| Detailed minute sample | SPY plus predeclared AAPL, AMZN, F, GE, JNJ, JPM, META, MSFT and XOM: 19,307,318 eligible minute rows. All ten show 3,436 observed dates, with matching paired raw daily dates. | The cohort was fixed before price checks, not selected from returns. It is not all stock/ETF minute archives or an eligibility-approved universe. |
| Actions | All four stock/ETF split/dividend ZIPs: 11,900 data members plus two README members. 396,030 date-prefix rows; 293,067 valid eligible actions plus two invalid zero-ratio rows. 96,840 earlier and 6,121 protected rows excluded before numeric decoding. | Date/value validity and within-file keys, not complete permanent-ID, announcement, payment or terminal-action evidence. |

The ten sampled minute files have no detected nonfinite/malformed numbers,
nonpositive OHLC, invalid OHLC bounds, negative/zero/fractional volume,
duplicate timestamps, ordering faults, malformed clocks, non-minute seconds
or weekend rows. The 19,307,318 timestamps were independently rescanned
without decoding any OHLCV by the timestamp supplement. All SPY and eight
stock observed dates contain every 10:00–10:05 slot; AMZN lacks three slots:
November 20, 2013 at 10:02; May 19, 2015 at 10:00; July 2, 2015 at 10:01.
This says nothing about whether a signal/order opportunity existed then.
Dependent completed-minute volume cannot be fabricated or forward-filled.

The paired raw daily checks also cover all 3,436 SPY dates. Those checks
find 20 fractional-volume rows in SPY, requiring volume-unit/source-contract
reconciliation; no other scalar/OHLCV/date fault is detected in the ten paired
daily files. This limited paired-file result does not supersede the full
stock-daily conflict census below or establish daily/minute price agreement.

There are 150 sample timestamps outside the diagnostic 04:00–20:00 range:
three at 03:59, 141 at 20:00 and six later. SPY has 38 dates with fewer than
390 observed minutes in the wall-clock 09:30–16:00 window; AMZN has 1,044.
These are reconciliation observations, not 38/1,044 proven missing sessions
or vendor errors. Early closes, halts, trade eligibility and source gaps need
their own evidence; extended-hours bars can occupy the afternoon after an
early close. No exact 210-minute shape safely identifies that calendar here.

`requirements.txt` pins `pandas_market_calendars==5.4.0`, but neither that
package nor `exchange_calendars` is available in the checked existing runtime
locations. No package is installed. Do not substitute weekdays, federal
holidays or the invented fixture calendar for a verified NYSE schedule.
Eastern start-of-bar documentation and available ZoneInfo support a later
declared conversion, not independent proof of the feed's timezone/availability.

### 59.3 Confirmed quality findings and reconciliation ledger

Counts below are input row/member occurrences; categories overlap. They are
not independent issuer/date defects, research samples, or a reason to remove
future losing securities. Resolve or quarantine affected observations under
frozen pre-outcome rules, not by averaging prices, choosing an arbitrary ZIP
copy, shifting dates or dropping inconvenient history.

| Finding | Count / disposition |
|---|---|
| Within-member daily duplicate timestamps | 6,914 rows. Of 1,464 differing raw representations, 735 are numeric OHLCV conflicts and 729 formatting-only. Numeric conflicts need canonical source/version resolution. |
| Cross-container daily duplicate timestamps | 434,575 occurrences, with 52,186 differing raw representations: **50,040 numeric conflicts**, 2,146 formatting-only. Do not equate a CRC or text difference with a numeric difference. |
| Nonpositive daily OHLC | 1,207 rows, all involving zero prices; no negative prices found. Active archives account for 1,034, delisted for 173. Fail dependent positive-price eligibility/valuation; do not silently substitute old prices. |
| Daily OHLC bounds | 721 violations, all in delisted archives. These require resolution or explicit refusal. |
| Daily volume | 6,593 fractional raw volumes (6,305 active), 370 zero-volume rows. Fractional values need source/normalization reconciliation with the integer-volume contract; zero daily volume is diagnostic, not automatically invalid. The README's omitted-zero claim is about intraday data. |
| Daily chronology / blank rows | Six order events; 44 date-prefix refusals were blank lines, not malformed calendar dates. No weekend, nonfinite, negative-volume, malformed-number, field-count or daily timestamp-format fault found. Post-protected-boundary chronology remains untested. |
| Splits | Two nonpositive ratios in `CTM.txt`, dated January 31, 2013 and May 14, 2015. Quarantine affected split records rather than guess an adjustment. Eligibility/type mapping determines which studies can encounter them. |
| Action dates / ordering | A HON split dated June 28, 2026 and MFICL dividend dated March 1, 2025 fall on weekends; effective-date/security-type reconciliation is needed, not automatic shifting. Seventeen ETF dividend files have mixed date order. Twenty-five zero cash amounts are flagged, not automatically invalid. |
| Action key validity | No malformed/nonfinite/negative-cash or duplicate/conflicting date keys within individual action files after separating README metadata. This is not cross-ticker/permanent-ID reconciliation. |

All within-member daily duplicate/order faults and all bounds violations occur
in delisted archives. Filtering only to surviving clean names would conceal
these risks. The broad daily audit includes instruments not yet demonstrated
to satisfy baseline common-share eligibility; it does not require repairing
every irrelevant warrant before a properly frozen study.

Central metadata also finds 384 active/delisted base-symbol overlaps after
stripping `-DELISTED`. Stock action filename joins leave 15 split and 224
dividend names unmatched, and 160/83 ambiguous active/delisted bases.
Punctuation variants, ETF members and minute-only symbols explain some cases;
they are not automatically missing actions. Active minute-only symbols are
ALCE, CRKN, DCOMG, IMPH, QH, VHUB and WHLM; MESA is daily-only. The ticker
list lacks permanent issuer/security IDs, instrument eligibility, currency,
dated exchange/sector/status and source publication/receipt clocks.

SPY has 54 eligible dividend records, March 15, 2013–June 18, 2026: four per
year through 2025 and two before the 2026 cutoff. There is no SPY split member;
absence is consistent with the file convention, not independent proof of a
complete split history. Two-column action files lack payment dates, currency,
permanent IDs, action/version IDs and availability clocks. An ex-date cannot
be treated as spendable dividend cash. Merger/spinoff/delisting consideration
and residual settlement economics remain unavailable/unverified.

### 59.4 Auditor controls, integrity and retained receipts

Author QA caught and corrected an audit-counter defect before final reporting:
daily v1's global-overlap counter included some repetitions within the first
container. An invented ZIP control fails that version; archive-aware v2 passes
and the full daily audit was rerun. V1 scripts/receipts are retained and
superseded, not erased. Raw text conflicts were then compared as exact Decimal
values to separate formatting-only differences. The semantic pass covers 749
target member occurrences / 1,062,685 eligible rows. All 44 apparent invalid
date rows were confirmed blank; two action README files were separated from
data rather than labelled malformed events.

One initial format probe emitted short prefixes containing a few price
lexemes from two eligible, unprotected rows (September 24, 2014 and April 7,
2025). This was disclosed and output was corrected; no protected financial
value or return was emitted. Do not claim zero raw price-lexeme exposure for
this round. Retained results contain aggregate diagnostics and dates only.

Minute parser controls pass six invented methods plus its original control
matrix (gates, duplicate semantics, finiteness, bounds, volume, clock/order).
Action controls pass five retained invented gate/scalar checks. The timestamp
supplement proves financial-payload changes do not change its output. Daily
invented ZIP controls cover gates, numeric/OHLCV validity, duplicates/source
overlap, order, ZIP-magic dispatch and CRC scope. These are author/subagent QA,
not independent Claude acceptance or a full lane/repository suite.

### 60.2 Implemented pure raw-source adapter and reconciliation contract

New `research/guidance_revision_drift/firstrate.py` implements:

- `parse_line`: bounded raw bytes, explicit vendor symbol/kind/source/member/
  line number, date gate before financial decoding or hashing. The maximum
  window remains `2013-01-01 <= date < 2026-09-01`; callers may narrow it.
  Known blank ASCII lines are retained `blank_nondata_line` exclusions, not
  guessed data or source-set poison. Nonblank unknown dates remain refusals.
- Explicit raw daily/minute member naming and exact plain/scientific Decimal
  normalization. No binary float, rounded fractional volume, filled price,
  adjusted-member substitution, source-data rewrite or quote fabrication.
  Finiteness, bounded precision/exponent/magnitude, positive OHLC, bounds,
  nonnegative integral volume and positive minute volume are enforced.
  Zero daily volume is retained with a warning, not declared valid liquidity.
- Immutable eligible-only line hashes and source references. Numeric-identical
  duplicates collapse while retaining every distinct source location. Any
  conflicting numeric copy or invalid known key quarantines the entire key;
  a later good duplicate cannot revive it. No archive precedence is invented.
  Nonblank unkeyed refusals block use of the supplied source set via `require`.
- Strict checked `FirstRateLine`, `ReconciledRow`, `QuarantinedRow` and
  `Reconciliation` values. Tuple/type, key partition, ordering, source-location
  identity and bounded input-count validation prevents public construction
  from bypassing the reconciler. Exact re-delivery remains idempotent while
  its input count is retained.
- New York period-start minute conversion, including explicit refusal of
  ambiguous/nonexistent DST clocks. `regular_minute` requires an externally
  supplied `CalendarSession` and explicit security ID, and returns a complete
  in-session raw `HistoricalMinute`; no holiday/half-day or identity is
  inferred. There is no NBBO, execution price, order, cash posting or native
  route in this API.
- Split effective dates and dividend ex-dates retain explicit missing payment
  dates/currency/permanent identity/availability. Action rows never become
  payable cash merely because a scalar parses. Warnings remain visible.

Every FirstRate audit/reconciliation projection remains provider `firstrate`,
Raw/non-filled, with PIT,
coverage, calendar/reference/availability verification, execution and native
claims false. The returned `HistoricalMinute` projection instead carries its
source hash and supplied identity/session plus raw/non-filled fields; it does
not carry provider/PIT verification flags or certify provenance. Its source
hash binds the FirstRate references; use does not relabel them as Massive.
A source ID/hash binds supplied bytes/lineage, not provider truth, completeness
or original availability. The caller must bind the whole
selected inventory and retain chronology diagnostics. Exact vendor symbols,
including `-DELISTED`, remain distinct; no suffix, punctuation or latest-ticker
join is a permanent-security mapping.

The new module is explicitly added to the offline review bundle inventory;
the fresh-process import guard includes it. Original Massive-shaped parsing,
SYN input contracts, strategy/sizing/fill/settlement/SPY rules, native import
closure, candidate and fixed 372-frame fixture are unchanged. This is an
actual local normalization API used by the retained reconciliation driver,
not a downloader or an empirical-ready/native adapter claim.

Public primary-source refresh supplies two positive existing-access leads:

- [EODHD corporate actions](https://eodhd.com/financial-apis/api-splits-dividends)
  explicitly includes All World. JSON fields include `paymentDate`, declaration/
  record dates, currency and `unadjustedValue`; `value` is split-adjusted.
  Prefer raw historical cash for raw-share accounting, and test null dates/
  join coverage rather than assuming fields are populated. CSV omits extended
  JSON fields. This is a documented route, not a completed stock/SPY row audit.
- [Massive dividends](https://massive.com/docs/rest/stocks/corporate-actions/dividends)
  lists Starter history from January 2000 with raw `cash_amount`, currency,
  `pay_date`, declaration/record dates and IDs, separate from adjusted cash.
  Existing Starter may close payment/currency gaps without an upgrade; actual
  coverage and raw-versus-adjusted agreement with the USB still need samples.
- [Sharadar actions](https://sharadar.com/docs/actions) includes ticker changes,
  splits/dividends/spinoffs, listing/delisting/reasons/acquisition counterparties
  and issuer relationships. These are useful terminal/reference metadata,
  not proof of full cash/stock consideration or settlement timing.
  [TICKERS](https://sharadar.com/docs/tickers) supplies permaticker/FIGI/type/
  currency/exchange/sector as a current snapshot, not a dated sector history.
- [FMP dividends](https://site.financialmodelingprep.com/developer/docs/stable/dividends-company)
  documents payment/record/declaration dates and adjusted/raw dividends;
  [calendar tiers](https://site.financialmodelingprep.com/datasets/calendars)
  place general calendar access at Premium. No unsupported Starter entitlement
  or upgrade recommendation is inferred.

also requires a version/unit binding. EODHD's [US live EDGX feed](https://eodhd.com/financial-apis/new-real-time-data-api-websockets)
is not consolidated historical NBBO. Analyst estimates/ratings and USB earnings

control through the isolated browser. Credential-bearing temporary snapshot
text is cleared and extra dashboard tabs are closed. No secret is committed.

### 62.2 Actual requests, accepted samples and limits

Sixteen bounded GET requests use only `/api/div/{SPY.US,AAPL.US}` and
`/api/news` on `https://eodhd.com`, with no redirects followed, one MiB maximum
per response and no automatic retries. No QC, Massive, FMP or Sharadar API
request occurs. Every request is within the existing unprotected date fence;
records must pass complete date/ticker checks before financial conversion or
article analysis. JSON numeric lexemes remain strings until the private
Decimal boundary. Licensed response data is retained only in ignored private
artifacts, separately from the secret-free request context. No key is included.

| Request scope | Measured result | What it establishes / does not establish |
| --- | --- | --- |
| SPY, calendar 2025 | HTTP 200, four dividend rows, 745 bytes | All four payment/declaration/record dates, currency and raw cash fields populated; no sampled chronology flag. Not full-universe action coverage or PIT. |
| AAPL, calendar 2025 | HTTP 200, four rows, 721 bytes | Same field result; raw response SHA is byte-exact to section 61's public-demo sample, now independently retrieved using the account key. |
| JNJ, January 2025 | Five HTTP 200 pages, offsets 0/20/40/60/80, 98 rows; final page has 18 | Account news access and full content in this delivered window. Pagination end is not an issuer-disclosure completeness certificate. |
| JNJ, April 2025 | Five HTTP 200 pages, same offsets, 100 rows | Explicit audit cap reached. April remains partial; no absence or complete-history claim. |
| SPY and AAPL, 2020--2025 | Two HTTP 200 responses, 24 dividend rows each; four ex-dates per year per symbol | All 48 payment/declaration/record dates and currency/raw-cash fields populated and valid; raw and adjusted cash finite; no sampled date-order flags. Establishes six-calendar-year depth for these two returned series, not every issuer/action, permanent-ID mapping, original announcement availability or confirmation rights. |
| JNJ, January 2020 | HTTP 200, empty array, two bytes | Valid empty response; not evidence of usable guidance or an entitlement denial. |
| JNJ, April 2020 | HTTP 200 path reaches `news_record_symbol_mismatch` | Rejected before financial/content analysis. Exact payload/row count/hash were not retained by that client error path. Identity/filter diagnosis remains unresolved; no inferred coverage or attempted numeric repair. |

## 64. Exploratory guidance corpus and existing quote-rights audit, 2026-11-04

### 64.1 Owner scope, publication and evidence class

Owner: **"Proceed with building a usable guidance-revision dataset and
verifying historical stock/SPY bid/ask coverage and licensing using my existing
subscriptions. Don't access QC or buy anything, and push once when done."**
This round implements a pure local source compiler, builds an initial original-
disclosure corpus and refreshes existing-access/official coverage and terms.
It does not calculate real guidance percentage changes, signal thresholds,
trade eligibility, returns or portfolio outcomes. The evidence class remains
declared current-vintage historical reconstruction, `point_in_time_data=false`.
It is not a frozen research universe or permission to evaluate the strategy.

Start clean local and live matching remote
`5c2bcdcce97a12969afbb5b60e30fe7265b376f8`, mandatory worktree/branch/origin
verified. The section-63 publication is complete and is not repeated. No new
Claude commit is consumed; earlier review dispositions remain unchanged.
Only the lane module, its explicit review inventory, focused tests and this
record change. Root/shared documents stay frozen. Monitor remains PAUSED.

### 64.2 Compiler contract and limits

`guidance_dataset.py` compiles explicit source captures and canonical source
profiles into `gdr.guidance-dataset.v1`. It checks exact retained bytes/hashes,
primary URI/capture metadata, field-value hashes and locators, declared issuer/
FY/publication inventory, ordered finite bounds, units, accounting/adjustment,
share and scope labels. It checks immediate **supplied** predecessor order;
the declared inventory is not proof that all issuer disclosures were found.
Unknowns, missing releases, unchanged ranges, corrections, withdrawals and
incompatible/expired fiscal guidance remain visible. It never forward-fills
missing full-year bounds or calls the strategy's revision/eligibility routines.

The immutable public constructor rechecks the same inventories as the compiler;
deserialization requires a separately retained expected hash. A current source
capture is never backdated into a historical delivery. The safe summary exposes
counts/hashes/refusal reasons without financial bounds. All PIT, historical-
availability, source-completeness/authenticity certification, rights, approved-
strategy-input and execution gates are fixed Boolean false. Source locators
and semantic interpretations remain author assertions, not proof minted by a
matching hash. The module has no provider, file, order or native-engine actuator.

Complete issuer presentation originals require 32 MiB per capture / 96 MiB
total local memory bounds; the actual off-cycle PDFs include approximately
21.6 MB and 27.3 MB originals. The canonical metadata/profile dataset remains
capped at 4 MiB. These new local audit bounds do not change any native bundle,
cloud quota, log transport or recovery candidate. No excerpt is represented
as an original, and no platform flag or limit is bypassed. The new module is
added explicitly to `bundle._MODULES`; the native recovery carrier is not rebuilt.

### 64.3 Original-source reconstruction and conservative use

The source scope was chosen before inspecting bounds: Adobe FY2025's initial
December 2024 release and March/June/September 2025 releases, extended to the
contiguous FY2024 chain. The publication audit window is December 2023 through
September 2025. Eight scheduled issuer originals, two issuer-prepared remarks,
the FY2024 annual report and three dated FY2025 quarterly reports are retained.
The known intervening March 26, 2024 and March 18, 2025 investor meetings and
October 14, 2024 MAX material are then added to the source/chain inventory.
This remains a bounded corpus, not an exhaustive off-cycle census or six-year
guidance dataset, and it is not selected by price performance or threshold raises.

Quarter-only releases retain explicit missing full-year fields. Initial versus
later non-GAAP definition changes are preserved as comparability refusals;
matching revenue/EPS labels alone do not override them. The image-based March
2025 investor deck required local Apple Vision OCR: 109 pages completed without
OCR errors, but OCR success alone was not numeric acceptance. Root rendered and
visually inspected its page 7 and March 2024's page 8, including full-year
headings, total-revenue versus segment/ARR rows, GAAP versus non-GAAP EPS and
diluted-share footnotes. Private exact checks confirm all four March 2025 bounds
match the March 12 original and all four March 2024 bounds match the December
2023 original. Known reaffirmations are retained rather than skipped when
determining later supplied predecessors. The MAX material's historical FY
columns do not manufacture a fresh positive guidance event.

Identity uses a declared issuer SEC-file-number/common-stock-class namespace,
not a claimed CUSIP/FIGI/CIK mapping. USD normalization uses explicit U.S.-dollar
reporting context joined to the issuer target table, not a dollar sign alone.
Fiscal dates derived from the issuer's documented Friday-nearest-November-30
calendar policy are labelled as calendar derivations; as-presented diluted
share basis is not a constant share count or verified split-adjusted series.
These qualifications and source locators remain in the private profiles.
They do not establish dated universe membership or complete acquisition/
correction/withdrawal history. Publication dates without reliable times remain
date-only. No first subscriber receipt, immutable historical version, six-year
coverage, execution timing, licensing certification or strategy acceptance is
inferred from a structurally comparable exploratory pair.

Final v2 retains **17 original PDFs / 63,295,358 bytes and 11 profiles**:
eight scheduled releases and three known off-cycle presentations. Nine profiles
contain full-year ranges; two quarter-only profiles retain missing full-year
fields. The compiled 78,601-byte private dataset has two baselines, three
structurally comparable links and six refusals, with zero missing profiles in
the explicitly declared eleven-event inventory. The comparable supplied chain
is March 12 -> March 18 -> June 12 -> September 11, 2025. March 18 republishes
unchanged targets and is **not** a fresh economic candidate; three comparable
links are not three qualifying raises or three independent events. Known
definition changes, missing predecessors and historical republication remain
refusals. This does not certify the complete issuer archive or an eligible
trading dataset.

the public-text level. Actual operative account and source-specific terms,

no agreement is accepted here and the public page is not an account grant.

Massive's current raw-retention text distinguishes data included under a new
plan from data whose licence ends. For affected raw data, stop use and delete
within 30 days; nonreconstructible research outputs may remain and unused
automatic backups have a separate maximum 90-day allowance. Cancellation does
not itself end the already-paid period. The quote size migration also requires
capture/version/unit binding: current REST shares cannot justify a blanket

### 64.5 Verified author defects and focused validation

| Finding | Priority/status | Evidence and correction |
| --- | --- | --- |
| GDR-RP64-001 | P2 / corrected before publication | Direct canonical-dataset construction originally skipped compiler inventory/scope/source-binding checks. Shared inventory validation now checks both construction paths, with forged/missing/duplicate/out-of-scope regressions. No pre-fix runtime failure count is invented. |
| GDR-RP64-002 | P3 / corrected before publication | Requiring a fiscal-year label to equal its calendar end year was not an original plan rule. Preserve the explicit label independently while validating the full-year interval; focused test uses a differing label/end year. |
| GDR-RP64-003 | P2 / corrected | Lexical timestamp sorting could place a later fractional instant before its predecessor; equivalent UTC spellings also escaped duplicate detection. Two invented public-path probes demonstrated false acceptance and two dedicated tests failed before correction. Sort/deduplicate parsed UTC instants; corrected tests pass. |
| GDR-RP64-004 | P2 / corrected before publication | Python equality allowed numeric zero to compare equal to false gates. Require each gate to be exactly Boolean false. Corrected public-constructor probe passes; pre-fix runtime acceptance was not observed and is not claimed. |
| GDR-RP64-005 | P2 / corrected | A source can contain an expired FY outlook, but it cannot form an eligible unexpired pair. One focused regression failed before correction; now retain the profile and emit a per-event expiry refusal. |

Initial system Python 3.9 cannot import slotted dataclasses, so that probe ran

local record commit `01b856be` one ahead. That commit is reviewed here and
published by this round's single push, as 64.7 anticipated.

network-denied and used invented rows only. Claude did not contact QC, a broker,
a provider or a provider account, and did not read the USB disk or Codex's
ignored private artifacts. Provider, USB and corpus results in sections 57
through 64 are therefore checked here for internal arithmetic and consistency
only; their measured values rest on Codex's retained receipts.

### 65.2 Findings ledger

| ID | Priority/status | Location, evidence and correction |
|---|---|---|
| GDR-CR65-001 | P2 / corrected | `firstrate.py` constructors. Only `parse_line` checked the member name. A directly built row from `SYN_full_1min_ADJUSTED.txt` or `OTHER_full_1min_UNADJUSTED.txt` reconciled, projected `normalization: Raw` and reached `regular_minute`, contradicting 60.2's claim that public construction cannot bypass the reconciler. Action files were not bound at all: a dividend file parsed as `kind="split"` was accepted, so a cash amount could be read as a split ratio. One member rule now covers `parse_line`, `FirstRateLine`, `ReconciledRow` and `QuarantinedRow`, for every source of a row. |
| GDR-CR65-002 | P2 / corrected | `guidance_dataset._decisions`. A date-only row carries an issuer-local date, but `_profile` requires an instant's date to be UTC. A January 2 date-only row followed by a January 2 evening New York release, stored as January 3 UTC, sorted as a clean pair with no ambiguity. This is the class of defect GDR-RP64-003 rated P2. Rows within one day across the two clock kinds now refuse with `ambiguous_publication_order`; the refusal is conservative and adds no financial arithmetic. |
| GDR-CR65-003 | P3 / corrected | `test_pre_qc.py`. `test_actual_handoff_reconstructs_complete_source_and_two_closed_plans` has failed since `3e144031` and still failed at `88a35c0c`: it pinned 61 members while the source builds 62, then 63. The handoff itself was right. Codex's focused closures did not include this file, repeating GDR-CR31-001 and GDR-CR47-001. The pin now derives from `source_manifest()`, so the next module cannot break it. |
| GDR-CR65-004 | P3 / corrected | `firstrate.py`. Direct construction could drop required diagnostics, including `dividend_payment_date_missing` and `zero_dividend_requires_reconciliation`. One `_required_warnings` definition now serves parsing and both constructors. |
| GDR-CR65-005 | P3 / corrected | `firstrate.py`. `repr` of `FirstRateLine` and `ReconciledRow` printed licensed values, unlike the compiler's `repr=False` bytes. Values are excluded from `repr`; projections are unchanged. |
| GDR-CR65-006 | P3 / corrected | `firstrate.parse_line`. Bytes containing an interior line terminator were hashed as one eligible row even when a later row carried a protected date, against the module's date-gate-before-hash contract. They now refuse unhashed and unkeyed as `embedded_line_terminator`, which blocks the source set. |
| GDR-CR65-007 | P3 / corrected | `firstrate._member`. `./x`, `a/./x` and `x/.` were accepted, so one member could occupy two source locations and pass as a duplicate. Dot segments now refuse. |
| GDR-CR65-008 | P3 / corrected | `guidance_dataset._uri`. `https://[bad` raised plain `ValueError`, and an NFKC-invalid host raised a message that echoed the location, against the module's static-refusal contract. Both now raise the static refusal; DEL is refused with the other control characters. |
| GDR-CR65-009 | P3 / corrected | `guidance_dataset._profile`. A list or object in a binding's `field` raised `TypeError`. It is now the named refusal. |
| GDR-CR65-010 | P3 / corrected | `compile_guidance_dataset`. Scope decoding used the 64 KiB default, so an inventory inside `MAX_PROFILES` could be refused with a generic error, while the public constructor accepted the same scope. Scope decoding now uses the dataset limit. |
| GDR-CR65-011 | P3 / corrected | Record line "Current scope/evidence are sections 17 through 63" missed section 64. It is corrected, and `test_boundaries.py` now derives the expected number from the latest section heading. |
| GDR-CR65-012 | P3 / corrected | Test adequacy across `firstrate.py`, `guidance_dataset.py`, `multi_session.py` and `pre_qc.py`; counts are in 65.4. Pinned behavior includes `regular_minute` lineage and close values, zero-price refusal, OHLC bounds for close, every unsupported unit and basis, baseline missing fields, retained-hash mismatch and the unfinished SPY entry. |
| GDR-CR65-013 | P3 / clarified | GDR-CCR55-002 says section 55 establishes no waiver of a Claude full-lane obligation. For this round the waiver is the owner's direct instruction quoted in 65.1, given in Claude's channel, so this review runs focused checks only. Under GDR-CCR42-002 the same rule runs both ways: each agent records direct human messages the other cannot see. |
| GDR-CR65-014 | P3 / documented | 62.1 and the status block say no EODHD key "was displayed or saved", yet 62.1 also takes the key from the dashboard's displayed token control through the browser and clears "credential-bearing temporary snapshot text". Claude cannot see Codex's tool output and does not claim a leak. Codex should state whether that snapshot reached model-visible output and, if so, qualify the claim as 60.5 did for Sharadar. |
| GDR-CR65-015 | P3 / documented | `published_date` has two meanings: issuer-local when date-only and UTC when an instant is present. GDR-CR65-002 contains the ordering effect. `fiscal_year_expired_at_publication` and the scope window still use the mixed value, and an evening release cannot state its local date. A later adapter needs an explicit issuer time zone. Not fixed: that is a schema decision for the corpus author. |
| GDR-CR65-016 | P3 / documented | GDR-RP64-002 removed the rule that a fiscal label equals its end year, but `guidance_inputs._period` still requires it. A compiled pair for a fiscal year ending after its label would be refused downstream. No bridge exists yet, so nothing is wrong today. The original plan's rule should be settled before one is built. |

Scripted count from this table: P0 0, P1 0, P2 2, P3 14; corrected 12, clarified 1, documented 3.

### 65.4 Mutation and focused verification

All mutants were rewritten in memory; no source file was changed by a run.

Each remaining survivor was run and has a stated reason. FirstRate's 21: regexes
already used through `fullmatch` or rejected by the next check; `UnicodeError`
being a `ValueError`; the DST offset guard making the round-trip check and
`later` equivalent; constructor rechecks returning the same code; defensive
error-text cleanup with no reachable input; and count, key and location rules
refusing what an ordering or size check would. The compiler's 17: empty or
malformed inventories refused by the next check, hash equality covering the
digest check, one of two identical `current_missing` loops, and compiler
rechecks repeated by `GuidanceDataset`. The engine's one: `released >= ZERO`,
which cannot go negative because a buy never fills above its limit.

The ten `ReviewCorrectionTests` methods all fail on Codex's published source,
with 52 failures across methods and subtests, and pass after correction. Each
corrected behavior also has a mutant caught on the corrected source.
